--- floydwarshall files ---
./floydwarshall/floydwarshall.cpp
--- ./floydwarshall/floydwarshall.cpp
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

codex
The benchmark stores each matrix column contiguously, so I’m using a 1D block-column decomposition: each rank updates its owned columns, and the current intermediate column is broadcast once per Floyd–Warshall iteration. This keeps the inner update loop contiguous and avoids replicating the full matrix on non-root ranks; rank 0 gathers the final matrix only when required for validation or result reporting.
The source uses column-major storage, which maps naturally to a block-column MPI decomposition with one pivot-column broadcast per iteration.
  ✓ Inspect the benchmark source, input/output semantics, and CMake configuration
  → Implement distributed-memory MPI parallelization in the existing files
  • Build and run correctness/performance sanity checks

+void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes,
+                          const size_t firstColumn, const size_t numColumns) {
+    // The original matrix is column-major and path[i,j] is initialized to j.
+    // Initialize only the columns owned by this rank.
+    for (size_t localColumn = 0; localColumn < numColumns; ++localColumn) {
+        const unsigned int column = static_cast<unsigned int>(firstColumn + localColumn);
+        unsigned int* pathColumn = path.data() + localColumn * numNodes;
+        std::fill_n(pathColumn, numNodes, column);

-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
-                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
-                   const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                   const size_t numNodes,
+                   const size_t firstColumn,
+                   const size_t numColumns,
+                   const std::vector<size_t>& columnStarts,
+                   const int rank,
+                   const int worldSize) {
+    // The matrix is distributed in contiguous blocks of columns.  For each k,
+    // the owner broadcasts D[:,k], after which every rank updates its columns
+    // independently.  This is the column-major equivalent of the original
+    // i/j loop nest and requires only O(n) communication per iteration.

+void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes,
+                          const size_t firstColumn, const size_t numColumns) {
+    // The original matrix is column-major and path[i,j] is initialized to j.
+    // Initialize only the columns owned by this rank.
+    for (size_t localColumn = 0; localColumn < numColumns; ++localColumn) {
+        const unsigned int column = static_cast<unsigned int>(firstColumn + localColumn);
+        unsigned int* pathColumn = path.data() + localColumn * numNodes;
+        std::fill_n(pathColumn, numNodes, column);
     }

-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
-                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
-                   const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                   const size_t numNodes,
+                   const size_t firstColumn,
+                   const size_t numColumns,
+                   const std::vector<size_t>& columnStarts,
+                   const int rank,
+                   const int worldSize) {
+    // The matrix is distributed in contiguous blocks of columns.  For each k,
+    // the owner broadcasts D[:,k], after which every rank updates its columns
+    // independently.  This is the column-major equivalent of the original
+    // i/j loop nest and requires only O(n) communication per iteration.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumn(numNodes);
+
     for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(
+            std::upper_bound(columnStarts.begin(), columnStarts.end(), k) -
+            columnStarts.begin() - 1);
+
+        if (rank == owner) {
+            const size_t localPivotColumn = k - firstColumn;
+            std::memcpy(pivotColumn.data(),
+                        dist.data() + localPivotColumn * numNodes,
+                        numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));
+        }
+
+        MPI_Bcast(pivotColumn.data(), static_cast<int>(numNodes), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                  owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+        for (size_t localColumn = 0; localColumn < numColumns; ++localColumn) {
+            unsigned int* distanceColumn = dist.data() + localColumn * numNodes;
+            unsigned int* pathColumn = path.data() + localColumn * numNodes;
+            const unsigned int distIK = distanceColumn[k];
+
             for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {

-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivotColumn[j];
+                if (newDist < distanceColumn[j]) {
+                    distanceColumn[j] = newDist;
+                    pathColumn[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
                 }
             }
         }
     }

+size_t columnStart(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int worldSize) {

+    const size_t extraColumns = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);

-        if (valid) {
-            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
-            return 0;
-        } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
-            return 1;
+    columnStarts.back() = numNodes;
+
+    std::vector<int> elementCounts(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    std::vector<int> elementDisplacements(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    for (int process = 0; process < worldSize; ++process) {
+        const size_t processCount = columnCount(numNodes, process, worldSize) * numNodes;
+        const size_t processOffset = columnStart(numNodes, process, worldSize) * numNodes;
+        elementCounts[static_cast<size_t>(process)] = static_cast<int>(processCount);
+        elementDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(process)] = static_cast<int>(processOffset);
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        MPI_Scatterv(dist.data(), elementCounts.data(), elementDisplacements.data(),
+                     MPI_UNSIGNED, MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    } else {
+        MPI_Scatterv(nullptr, nullptr, nullptr, MPI_UNSIGNED, dist.data(),
+                     static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+

+        printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");

+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();

+                  columnStarts, rank, worldSize);
+
+    const double elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double computationTime = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&elapsed, &computationTime, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        MPI_Gatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_UNSIGNED, dist.data(),
+                    elementCounts.data(), elementDisplacements.data(),
+                    MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    } else {
+        MPI_Gatherv(dist.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                    nullptr, nullptr, nullptr, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const long long milliseconds = static_cast<long long>(computationTime * 1000.0);
+        printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);
+
+        // Floyd-Warshall has O(n^3) complexity.
+        const double ops = static_cast<double>(numNodes) * numNodes * numNodes;
+        const double gflops = computationTime > 0.0
+                                  ? ops / computationTime / 1e9
+                                  : 0.0;
+        printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
         }
+
+        if (validate) {
+            printf("Validating result...\n");
+            const bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
+
+            if (valid) {
+                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {
+                printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+            }
+            MPI_Finalize();
+            return valid ? 0 : 1;
+        }
     }

+
+    MPI_Finalize();
     return 0;

-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,

-                   const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm

+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                   const size_t numNodes,
+                   const size_t firstColumn,
+                   const size_t numColumns,
+                   const std::vector<size_t>& columnStarts,
+                   const int rank,
+                   const int /*worldSize*/) {
+    // The matrix is distributed in contiguous blocks of columns.  For each k,
+    // the owner broadcasts D[:,k], after which every rank updates its columns
+    // independently.  This is the column-major equivalent of the original
+    // i/j loop nest and requires only O(n) communication per iteration.

-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {

+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(
+            std::upper_bound(columnStarts.begin(), columnStarts.end(), k) -
+            columnStarts.begin() - 1);
+
+        if (rank == owner) {
+            const size_t localPivotColumn = k - firstColumn;
+            std::memcpy(pivotColumn.data(),
+                        dist.data() + localPivotColumn * numNodes,
+                        numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));

+                  owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+

+void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes,
+                          const size_t firstColumn, const size_t numColumns) {
+    // The original matrix is column-major and path[i,j] is initialized to j.
+    // Initialize only the columns owned by this rank.
+    for (size_t localColumn = 0; localColumn < numColumns; ++localColumn) {
+        const unsigned int column = static_cast<unsigned int>(firstColumn + localColumn);
+        unsigned int* pathColumn = path.data() + localColumn * numNodes;
+        std::fill_n(pathColumn, numNodes, column);

+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,

+                   const size_t firstColumn,
+                   const size_t numColumns,
+                   const std::vector<size_t>& columnStarts,
+                   const int rank,
+                   const int /*worldSize*/) {
+    // The matrix is distributed in contiguous blocks of columns.  For each k,
+    // the owner broadcasts D[:,k], after which every rank updates its columns
+    // independently.  This is the column-major equivalent of the original
+    // i/j loop nest and requires only O(n) communication per iteration.

+        if (rank == owner) {
+            const size_t localPivotColumn = k - firstColumn;
+            std::memcpy(pivotColumn.data(),
+                        dist.data() + localPivotColumn * numNodes,
+                        numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));

     }

+size_t columnStart(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int worldSize) {

-void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {

+void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes,
+                          const size_t firstColumn, const size_t numColumns) {
+    // The original matrix is column-major and path[i,j] is initialized to j.
+    // Initialize only the columns owned by this rank.
+    for (size_t localColumn = 0; localColumn < numColumns; ++localColumn) {

-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
-                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
-                   const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                   const size_t numNodes,
+                   const size_t firstColumn,
+                   const size_t numColumns,
+                   const std::vector<size_t>& columnStarts,
+                   const int rank) {
+    // The matrix is distributed in contiguous blocks of columns.  For each k,
+    // the owner broadcasts D[:,k], after which every rank updates its columns
+    // independently.  This is the column-major equivalent of the original
+    // i/j loop nest and requires only O(n) communication per iteration.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumn(numNodes);
+
     for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(
+            std::upper_bound(columnStarts.begin(), columnStarts.end(), k) -
+            columnStarts.begin() - 1);
+
+        if (rank == owner) {
+            const size_t localPivotColumn = k - firstColumn;
+            std::memcpy(pivotColumn.data(),
+                        dist.data() + localPivotColumn * numNodes,
+                        numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));
+        }
+
+        MPI_Bcast(pivotColumn.data(), static_cast<int>(numNodes), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                  owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+        for (size_t localColumn = 0; localColumn < numColumns; ++localColumn) {
+            unsigned int* distanceColumn = dist.data() + localColumn * numNodes;
+            unsigned int* pathColumn = path.data() + localColumn * numNodes;
+            const unsigned int distIK = distanceColumn[k];
+
             for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivotColumn[j];
+                if (newDist < distanceColumn[j]) {
+                    distanceColumn[j] = newDist;
+                    pathColumn[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
                 }
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
+size_t columnStart(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int worldSize) {
+    const size_t baseColumns = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t extraColumns = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    return static_cast<size_t>(rank) * baseColumns +
+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extraColumns);
+}
+
+size_t columnCount(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int worldSize) {
+    const size_t baseColumns = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t extraColumns = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    return baseColumns + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraColumns ? 1 : 0);
+}

+        const size_t processOffset = columnStart(numNodes, process, worldSize) * numNodes;

+

+                    rank == 0 ? elementCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? elementDisplacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const long long milliseconds = static_cast<long long>(computationTime * 1000.0);
+        printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);
+
+        // Floyd-Warshall has O(n^3) complexity.
+        const double ops = static_cast<double>(numNodes) * numNodes * numNodes;
+        const double gflops = computationTime > 0.0
+                                  ? ops / computationTime / 1e9
+                                  : 0.0;
+        printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results_int(globalDist, "DistanceMatrix");
+        }
+
+        if (validate) {
+            printf("Validating result...\n");
+            const bool valid = validateResult(globalDist, numNodes);
+
+            if (valid) {
+                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {
+                printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+            }
+            MPI_Finalize();
+            return valid ? 0 : 1;
+        }
     }

+                 elementCounts.data(), elementDisplacements.data(), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                 dist.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    std::vector<unsigned int>().swap(initialDist);
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
+    }
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
+
+    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes, firstColumn, numColumns,
+                  columnStarts, rank, worldSize);
+
+    const double elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double computationTime = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&elapsed, &computationTime, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    const bool needGlobalResult = printResults || validate;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> globalDist(
+        rank == 0 && needGlobalResult ? numNodes * numNodes : 0);
+    if (needGlobalResult) {
+        MPI_Gatherv(dist.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                    rank == 0 ? globalDist.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? elementCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? elementDisplacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const long long milliseconds = static_cast<long long>(computationTime * 1000.0);
+        printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);
+
+        // Floyd-Warshall has O(n^3) complexity.
+        const double ops = static_cast<double>(numNodes) * numNodes * numNodes;
+        const double gflops = computationTime > 0.0
+                                  ? ops / computationTime / 1e9
+                                  : 0.0;
+        printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results_int(globalDist, "DistanceMatrix");
+        }
+
+        if (validate) {
+            printf("Validating result...\n");
+            const bool valid = validateResult(globalDist, numNodes);
+
+            if (valid) {
+                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {
+                printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+            }
+            MPI_Finalize();
+            return valid ? 0 : 1;
+        }
     }
-    
+
+    MPI_Finalize();
     return 0;
 }

-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,

+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                   const size_t numNodes,
+                   const size_t firstColumn,
+                   const size_t numColumns,
+                   const std::vector<size_t>& columnStarts,
+                   const int rank) {
+    // The matrix is distributed in contiguous blocks of columns.  For each k,
+    // the owner broadcasts D[:,k], after which every rank updates its columns
+    // independently.  This is the column-major equivalent of the original
+    // i/j loop nest and requires only O(n) communication per iteration.

     for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {

+            std::upper_bound(columnStarts.begin(), columnStarts.end(), k) -

+        if (rank == owner) {
+            const size_t localPivotColumn = k - firstColumn;
+            std::memcpy(pivotColumn.data(),
+                        dist.data() + localPivotColumn * numNodes,
+                        numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include <mpi.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes,
                          const size_t firstColumn, const size_t numColumns) {
    // The original matrix is column-major and path[i,j] is initialized to j.
    // Initialize only the columns owned by this rank.
    for (size_t localColumn = 0; localColumn < numColumns; ++localColumn) {
        const unsigned int column = static_cast<unsigned int>(firstColumn + localColumn);
        unsigned int* pathColumn = path.data() + localColumn * numNodes;
        std::fill_n(pathColumn, numNodes, column);
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
                   const size_t numNodes,
                   const size_t firstColumn,
                   const size_t numColumns,
                   const std::vector<size_t>& columnStarts,
                   const int rank) {
    // The matrix is distributed in contiguous blocks of columns.  For each k,
    // the owner broadcasts D[:,k], after which every rank updates its columns
    // independently.  This is the column-major equivalent of the original
    // i/j loop nest and requires only O(n) communication per iteration.
    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumn(numNodes);

    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        const int owner = static_cast<int>(
            std::upper_bound(columnStarts.begin(), columnStarts.end(), k) -
            columnStarts.begin() - 1);

        if (rank == owner) {
            const size_t localPivotColumn = k - firstColumn;
            std::memcpy(pivotColumn.data(),
                        dist.data() + localPivotColumn * numNodes,
                        numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));
        }

        MPI_Bcast(pivotColumn.data(), static_cast<int>(numNodes), MPI_UNSIGNED,
                  owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

        for (size_t localColumn = 0; localColumn < numColumns; ++localColumn) {
            unsigned int* distanceColumn = dist.data() + localColumn * numNodes;
            unsigned int* pathColumn = path.data() + localColumn * numNodes;
            const unsigned int distIK = distanceColumn[k];

            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivotColumn[j];
                if (newDist < distanceColumn[j]) {
                    distanceColumn[j] = newDist;
                    pathColumn[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

size_t columnStart(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int worldSize) {
    const size_t baseColumns = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
    const size_t extraColumns = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
    return static_cast<size_t>(rank) * baseColumns +
           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extraColumns);
}

size_t columnCount(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int worldSize) {
    const size_t baseColumns = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
    const size_t extraColumns = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
    return baseColumns + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraColumns ? 1 : 0);
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);

    int rank = 0;
    int worldSize = 1;
    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);

    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            if (rank == 0) {
                printUsage(argv[0]);
            }
            MPI_Finalize();
            return 0;
        } else {
            if (rank == 0) {
                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
                printUsage(argv[0]);
            }
            MPI_Finalize();
            return 1;
        }
    }

    if (numNodes > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
        if (rank == 0) {
            printf("Number of nodes is too large for MPI counts\n");
        }
        MPI_Finalize();
        return 1;
    }

    if (numNodes != 0 &&
        numNodes > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / numNodes) {
        if (rank == 0) {
            printf("The matrix is too large to address\n");
        }
        MPI_Finalize();
        return 1;
    }

    const size_t firstColumn = columnStart(numNodes, rank, worldSize);
    const size_t numColumns = columnCount(numNodes, rank, worldSize);
    const size_t localElements = numColumns * numNodes;

    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
        printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
        printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    }

    // Every rank allocates only its owned columns.  Rank 0 uses a temporary
    // complete matrix as the deterministic initialization source and releases
    // it immediately after distribution.
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(localElements);
    std::vector<unsigned int> initialDist(rank == 0 ? numNodes * numNodes : 0);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(localElements);

    if (rank == 0) {
        initializeDistanceMatrix(initialDist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    }
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes, firstColumn, numColumns);

    std::vector<size_t> columnStarts(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize) + 1);
    for (int process = 0; process <= worldSize; ++process) {
        columnStarts[static_cast<size_t>(process)] =
            columnStart(numNodes, process, worldSize);
    }
    columnStarts.back() = numNodes;

    std::vector<int> elementCounts(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
    std::vector<int> elementDisplacements(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
    for (int process = 0; process < worldSize; ++process) {
        const size_t processCount = columnCount(numNodes, process, worldSize) * numNodes;
        const size_t processOffset = columnStart(numNodes, process, worldSize) * numNodes;
        if (processCount > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
            processOffset > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
            if (rank == 0) {
                printf("The matrix distribution is too large for MPI counts\n");
            }
            MPI_Finalize();
            return 1;
        }
        elementCounts[static_cast<size_t>(process)] = static_cast<int>(processCount);
        elementDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(process)] = static_cast<int>(processOffset);
    }

    MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? initialDist.data() : nullptr,
                 elementCounts.data(), elementDisplacements.data(), MPI_UNSIGNED,
                 dist.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_UNSIGNED,
                 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    std::vector<unsigned int>().swap(initialDist);

    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    }
    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    const double start = MPI_Wtime();

    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes, firstColumn, numColumns,
                  columnStarts, rank);

    const double elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
    double computationTime = 0.0;
    MPI_Reduce(&elapsed, &computationTime, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

    const bool needGlobalResult = printResults || validate;
    std::vector<unsigned int> globalDist(
        rank == 0 && needGlobalResult ? numNodes * numNodes : 0);
    if (needGlobalResult) {
        MPI_Gatherv(dist.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_UNSIGNED,
                    rank == 0 ? globalDist.data() : nullptr,
                    rank == 0 ? elementCounts.data() : nullptr,
                    rank == 0 ? elementDisplacements.data() : nullptr,
                    MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    }

    if (rank == 0) {
        const long long milliseconds = static_cast<long long>(computationTime * 1000.0);
        printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);

        // Floyd-Warshall has O(n^3) complexity.
        const double ops = static_cast<double>(numNodes) * numNodes * numNodes;
        const double gflops = computationTime > 0.0
                                  ? ops / computationTime / 1e9
                                  : 0.0;
        printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);

        if (printResults) {
            print_results_int(globalDist, "DistanceMatrix");
        }

        if (validate) {
            printf("Validating result...\n");
            const bool valid = validateResult(globalDist, numNodes);

            if (valid) {
                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            } else {
                printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            }
            MPI_Finalize();
            return valid ? 0 : 1;
        }
    }

    MPI_Finalize();
    return 0;
}

+                        dist.data() + localPivotColumn * numNodes,

+size_t columnStart(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int worldSize) {
+    const size_t baseColumns = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);

+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extraColumns);

+    return baseColumns + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraColumns ? 1 : 0);

+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                   const size_t numNodes,
+                   const size_t firstColumn,
+                   const size_t numColumns,
+                   const std::vector<size_t>& columnStarts,
+                   const int rank) {
+    // The matrix is distributed in contiguous blocks of columns.  For each k,
+    // the owner broadcasts D[:,k], after which every rank updates its columns
+    // independently.  This is the column-major equivalent of the original
+    // i/j loop nest and requires only O(n) communication per iteration.

+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(
+            std::upper_bound(columnStarts.begin(), columnStarts.end(), k) -
+            columnStarts.begin() - 1);
+
+        if (rank == owner) {
+            const size_t localPivotColumn = k - firstColumn;
+            std::memcpy(pivotColumn.data(),
+                        dist.data() + localPivotColumn * numNodes,
+                        numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));
+        }
+
+        MPI_Bcast(pivotColumn.data(), static_cast<int>(numNodes), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                  owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+            unsigned int* distanceColumn = dist.data() + localColumn * numNodes;

+                    distanceColumn[j] = newDist;
+                    pathColumn[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);

             }
         }
     }

+size_t columnStart(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int worldSize) {

+    const size_t extraColumns = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);

+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extraColumns);

+size_t columnCount(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int worldSize) {

+    const size_t extraColumns = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    return baseColumns + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraColumns ? 1 : 0);

+                          const size_t firstColumn, const size_t numColumns) {

+            } else {

codex
The working set is now genuinely distributed during the timed computation: rank 0 uses a temporary full matrix only to preserve the original deterministic initialization, then releases it; a full matrix is gathered only for `-r` or `-v`. Small-graph hashes match across 1, 2, 4, and 8 ranks, including an over-subscribed case with empty rank partitions. I’m doing larger sanity runs and a final source/build review.

-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,

+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,

+                   const size_t numNodes,
+                   const size_t firstColumn,
+                   const size_t numColumns,
+                   const std::vector<size_t>& columnStarts,
+                   const int rank) {
+    // The matrix is distributed in contiguous blocks of columns.  For each k,
+    // the owner broadcasts D[:,k], after which every rank updates its columns
+    // independently.  This is the column-major equivalent of the original
+    // i/j loop nest and requires only O(n) communication per iteration.

+            std::upper_bound(columnStarts.begin(), columnStarts.end(), k) -
+            columnStarts.begin() - 1);

+            const size_t localPivotColumn = k - firstColumn;
+            std::memcpy(pivotColumn.data(),
+                        dist.data() + localPivotColumn * numNodes,
+                        numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));
+        }

+        }
+        MPI_Finalize();
+        return 1;
+    }

+    const size_t firstColumn = columnStart(numNodes, rank, worldSize);

+    const size_t localElements = numColumns * numNodes;

+        printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
+        printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
+        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
+        printf("Initializing graph...\n");
+    }
+
+    // Every rank allocates only its owned columns.  Rank 0 uses a temporary
+    // complete matrix as the deterministic initialization source and releases
+    // it immediately after distribution.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(localElements);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> initialDist(rank == 0 ? numNodes * numNodes : 0);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> path(localElements);
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        initializeDistanceMatrix(initialDist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
+    }
+    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes, firstColumn, numColumns);
+
+    std::vector<size_t> columnStarts(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize) + 1);
+    for (int process = 0; process <= worldSize; ++process) {
+        columnStarts[static_cast<size_t>(process)] =
+            columnStart(numNodes, process, worldSize);
     }
-

-        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
-        
-        if (valid) {
-            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
-            return 0;
-        } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");

+
+    std::vector<int> elementCounts(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    std::vector<int> elementDisplacements(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    for (int process = 0; process < worldSize; ++process) {
+        const size_t processCount = columnCount(numNodes, process, worldSize) * numNodes;
+        const size_t processOffset = columnStart(numNodes, process, worldSize) * numNodes;
+        if (processCount > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            processOffset > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            if (rank == 0) {
+                printf("The matrix distribution is too large for MPI counts\n");
+            }
+            MPI_Finalize();
             return 1;
         }
+        elementCounts[static_cast<size_t>(process)] = static_cast<int>(processCount);
+        elementDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(process)] = static_cast<int>(processOffset);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? initialDist.data() : nullptr,
+                 elementCounts.data(), elementDisplacements.data(), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                 dist.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    std::vector<unsigned int>().swap(initialDist);

+    if (rank == 0) {
+        printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");

+                  columnStarts, rank);

+    const double elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double computationTime = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&elapsed, &computationTime, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    const bool needGlobalResult = printResults || validate;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> globalDist(
+        rank == 0 && needGlobalResult ? numNodes * numNodes : 0);
+    if (needGlobalResult) {
+        MPI_Gatherv(dist.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                    rank == 0 ? globalDist.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? elementCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? elementDisplacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const long long milliseconds = static_cast<long long>(computationTime * 1000.0);
+        printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);
+
+        // Floyd-Warshall has O(n^3) complexity.
+        const double ops = static_cast<double>(numNodes) * numNodes * numNodes;
+        const double gflops = computationTime > 0.0
+                                  ? ops / computationTime / 1e9
+                                  : 0.0;
+        printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results_int(globalDist, "DistanceMatrix");
+        }
+
+        if (validate) {
+            printf("Validating result...\n");
+            const bool valid = validateResult(globalDist, numNodes);
+
+            if (valid) {
+                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {

+    // The matrix is distributed in contiguous blocks of columns.  For each k,
+    // the owner broadcasts D[:,k], after which every rank updates its columns

+size_t columnStart(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int worldSize) {

+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const long long milliseconds = static_cast<long long>(computationTime * 1000.0);
+        printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);
+
+        // Floyd-Warshall has O(n^3) complexity.
+        const double ops = static_cast<double>(numNodes) * numNodes * numNodes;
+        const double gflops = computationTime > 0.0
+                                  ? ops / computationTime / 1e9
+                                  : 0.0;
+        printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);

+            print_results_int(globalDist, "DistanceMatrix");
+        }
+

+            printf("Validating result...\n");
+            const bool valid = validateResult(globalDist, numNodes);
+
+            if (valid) {
+                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {
+                printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+            }
+            MPI_Finalize();
+            return valid ? 0 : 1;
+        }

-    
+
+    MPI_Finalize();
     return 0;

+        MPI_Finalize();
+        return 1;
+    }

+    if (numNodes != 0 &&
+        numNodes > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / numNodes) {
+        if (rank == 0) {

+        }
+        MPI_Finalize();
+        return 1;

+
+    // Every rank allocates only its owned columns.  Rank 0 uses a temporary
+    // complete matrix as the deterministic initialization source and releases
+    // it immediately after distribution.

+    std::vector<unsigned int> initialDist(rank == 0 ? numNodes * numNodes : 0);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> path(localElements);

+        initializeDistanceMatrix(initialDist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
+    }
+    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes, firstColumn, numColumns);
+

+    for (int process = 0; process <= worldSize; ++process) {

-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");

+    for (int process = 0; process < worldSize; ++process) {
+        const size_t processCount = columnCount(numNodes, process, worldSize) * numNodes;
+        const size_t processOffset = columnStart(numNodes, process, worldSize) * numNodes;
+        if (processCount > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            processOffset > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {

+                printf("The matrix distribution is too large for MPI counts\n");

+                    rank == 0 ? elementCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? elementDisplacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+            print_results_int(globalDist, "DistanceMatrix");
+        }
+
+        if (validate) {
+            printf("Validating result...\n");
+            const bool valid = validateResult(globalDist, numNodes);
+
+            if (valid) {
+                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {

+            }

-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
-                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
-                   const size_t numNodes) {

+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                   const size_t numNodes,
+                   const size_t firstColumn,
+                   const size_t numColumns,
+                   const std::vector<size_t>& columnStarts,
+                   const int rank) {
+    // The matrix is distributed in contiguous blocks of columns.  For each k,
+    // the owner broadcasts D[:,k], after which every rank updates its columns
+    // independently.  This is the column-major equivalent of the original
+    // i/j loop nest and requires only O(n) communication per iteration.

     for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {

+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(
+            std::upper_bound(columnStarts.begin(), columnStarts.end(), k) -
+            columnStarts.begin() - 1);
+
+        if (rank == owner) {
+            const size_t localPivotColumn = k - firstColumn;
+            std::memcpy(pivotColumn.data(),
+                        dist.data() + localPivotColumn * numNodes,
+                        numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));
+        }
+
+        MPI_Bcast(pivotColumn.data(), static_cast<int>(numNodes), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                  owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+        for (size_t localColumn = 0; localColumn < numColumns; ++localColumn) {
+            unsigned int* distanceColumn = dist.data() + localColumn * numNodes;
+            unsigned int* pathColumn = path.data() + localColumn * numNodes;

+                    pathColumn[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);

             }
         }
     }

+size_t columnStart(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int worldSize) {
+    const size_t baseColumns = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t extraColumns = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    return static_cast<size_t>(rank) * baseColumns +
+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extraColumns);
+}
+
+size_t columnCount(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int worldSize) {
+    const size_t baseColumns = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t extraColumns = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    return baseColumns + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraColumns ? 1 : 0);
+}

project(floydwarshall_benchmark)

     1	#include <algorithm>
     2	#include <cstdio>
     3	#include <cstdlib>
     4	#include <cstring>

     6	#include <vector>
     7

    10	#include "../common/results_output.hpp"
    11	
    12	constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
    13	constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
    14	
    15	// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
    16	inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    17	    return j * n + i;
    18	}
    19	
    20	void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
    21	                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    22	    unsigned int seed = 42;
    23	    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    24	    
    25	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
    26	        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    27	    }
    28	    
    29	    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    30	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
    31	        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    32	    }
    33	}
    34	
    35	void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes,
    36	                          const size_t firstColumn, const size_t numColumns) {
    37	    // The original matrix is column-major and path[i,j] is initialized to j.
    38	    // Initialize only the columns owned by this rank.
    39	    for (size_t localColumn = 0; localColumn < numColumns; ++localColumn) {
    40	        const unsigned int column = static_cast<unsigned int>(firstColumn + localColumn);
    41	        unsigned int* pathColumn = path.data() + localColumn * numNodes;
    42	        std::fill_n(pathColumn, numNodes, column);
    43	    }
    44	}
    45	
    46	void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
    47	                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
    48	                   const size_t numNodes,
    49	                   const size_t firstColumn,
    50	                   const size_t numColumns,
    51	                   const std::vector<size_t>& columnStarts,
    52	                   const int rank) {
    53	    // The matrix is distributed in contiguous blocks of columns.  For each k,
    54	    // the owner broadcasts D[:,k], after which every rank updates its columns
    55	    // independently.  This is the column-major equivalent of the original
    56	    // i/j loop nest and requires only O(n) communication per iteration.
    57	    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumn(numNodes);
    58	
    59	    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
    60	        const int owner = static_cast<int>(
    61	            std::upper_bound(columnStarts.begin(), columnStarts.end(), k) -
    62	            columnStarts.begin() - 1);
    63	
    64	        if (rank == owner) {
    65	            const size_t localPivotColumn = k - firstColumn;
    66	            std::memcpy(pivotColumn.data(),
    67	                        dist.data() + localPivotColumn * numNodes,
    68	                        numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));
    69	        }
    70	
    71	        MPI_Bcast(pivotColumn.data(), static_cast<int>(numNodes), MPI_UNSIGNED,
    72	                  owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    73	
    74	        for (size_t localColumn = 0; localColumn < numColumns; ++localColumn) {
    75	            unsigned int* distanceColumn = dist.data() + localColumn * numNodes;
    76	            unsigned int* pathColumn = path.data() + localColumn * numNodes;
    77	            const unsigned int distIK = distanceColumn[k];
    78	
    79	            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
    80	                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivotColumn[j];
    81	                if (newDist < distanceColumn[j]) {
    82	                    distanceColumn[j] = newDist;
    83	                    pathColumn[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
    84	                }
    85	            }
    86	        }
    87	    }
    88	}
    89	
    90	size_t columnStart(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int worldSize) {
    91	    const size_t baseColumns = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
    92	    const size_t extraColumns = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
    93	    return static_cast<size_t>(rank) * baseColumns +
    94	           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extraColumns);
    95	}
    96	
    97	size_t columnCount(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int worldSize) {
    98	    const size_t baseColumns = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
    99	    const size_t extraColumns = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
   100	    return baseColumns + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraColumns ? 1 : 0);
   101	}
   102	
   103	bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
   104	    // Basic sanity checks
   105	    
   106	    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
   107	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
   108	        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
   109	            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
   110	            return false;
   111	        }
   112	    }
   113	    
   114	    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
   115	    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
   116	    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
   117	        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
   118	            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
   119	                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
   120	                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
   121	                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
   122	                
   123	                // Check for overflow before addition
   124	                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
   125	                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
   126	                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
   127	                               i, j, k);
   128	                        return false;
   129	                    }
   130	                }
   131	            }
   132	        }
   133	    }
   134	    
   135	    return true;
   136	}
   137	
   138	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   139	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   140	    printf("Options:\n");
   141	    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
   142	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   143	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   144	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   145	}

   147	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   148	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   149	
   150	    int rank = 0;
   151	    int worldSize = 1;

   153	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);
   154

   157	    bool printResults = false;
   158	    
   159	    // Parse command line arguments

   162	            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
   163	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   164	            validate = true;
   165	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   166	            printResults = true;
   167	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   168	            if (rank == 0) {
   169	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   170	            }
   171	            MPI_Finalize();
   172	            return 0;
   173	        } else {
   174	            if (rank == 0) {
   175	                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   176	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   177	            }
   178	            MPI_Finalize();
   179	            return 1;
   180	        }
   181	    }
   182	
   183	    if (numNodes > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
   184	        if (rank == 0) {
   185	            printf("Number of nodes is too large for MPI counts\n");
   186	        }
   187	        MPI_Finalize();
   188	        return 1;
   189	    }
   190	
   191	    if (numNodes != 0 &&
   192	        numNodes > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / numNodes) {
   193	        if (rank == 0) {
   194	            printf("The matrix is too large to address\n");
   195	        }
   196	        MPI_Finalize();
   197	        return 1;
   198	    }
   199	
   200	    const size_t firstColumn = columnStart(numNodes, rank, worldSize);
   201	    const size_t numColumns = columnCount(numNodes, rank, worldSize);
   202	    const size_t localElements = numColumns * numNodes;
   203	
   204	    if (rank == 0) {
   205	        printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
   206	        printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
   207	        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   208	        printf("Initializing graph...\n");
   209	    }
   210	
   211	    // Every rank allocates only its owned columns.  Rank 0 uses a temporary
   212	    // complete matrix as the deterministic initialization source and releases
   213	    // it immediately after distribution.
   214	    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(localElements);
   215	    std::vector<unsigned int> initialDist(rank == 0 ? numNodes * numNodes : 0);
   216	    std::vector<unsigned int> path(localElements);
   217	
   218	    if (rank == 0) {
   219	        initializeDistanceMatrix(initialDist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
   220	    }
   221	    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes, firstColumn, numColumns);
   222	
   223	    std::vector<size_t> columnStarts(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize) + 1);
   224	    for (int process = 0; process <= worldSize; ++process) {
   225	        columnStarts[static_cast<size_t>(process)] =
   226	            columnStart(numNodes, process, worldSize);
   227	    }
   228	    columnStarts.back() = numNodes;
   229	
   230	    std::vector<int> elementCounts(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
   231	    std::vector<int> elementDisplacements(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
   232	    for (int process = 0; process < worldSize; ++process) {
   233	        const size_t processCount = columnCount(numNodes, process, worldSize) * numNodes;
   234	        const size_t processOffset = columnStart(numNodes, process, worldSize) * numNodes;
   235	        if (processCount > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
   236	            processOffset > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
   237	            if (rank == 0) {
   238	                printf("The matrix distribution is too large for MPI counts\n");
   239	            }
   240	            MPI_Finalize();
   241	            return 1;
   242	        }
   243	        elementCounts[static_cast<size_t>(process)] = static_cast<int>(processCount);
   244	        elementDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(process)] = static_cast<int>(processOffset);
   245	    }
   246	
   247	    MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? initialDist.data() : nullptr,
   248	                 elementCounts.data(), elementDisplacements.data(), MPI_UNSIGNED,
   249	                 dist.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_UNSIGNED,
   250	                 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   251	    std::vector<unsigned int>().swap(initialDist);
   252	
   253	    if (rank == 0) {
   254	        printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
   255	    }
   256	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   257	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   258	
   259	    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes, firstColumn, numColumns,
   260	                  columnStarts, rank);
   261	
   262	    const double elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   263	    double computationTime = 0.0;
   264	    MPI_Reduce(&elapsed, &computationTime, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   265	
   266	    const bool needGlobalResult = printResults || validate;
   267	    std::vector<unsigned int> globalDist(
   268	        rank == 0 && needGlobalResult ? numNodes * numNodes : 0);
   269	    if (needGlobalResult) {
   270	        MPI_Gatherv(dist.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_UNSIGNED,
   271	                    rank == 0 ? globalDist.data() : nullptr,
   272	                    rank == 0 ? elementCounts.data() : nullptr,
   273	                    rank == 0 ? elementDisplacements.data() : nullptr,
   274	                    MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   275	    }
   276	
   277	    if (rank == 0) {
   278	        const long long milliseconds = static_cast<long long>(computationTime * 1000.0);
   279	        printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);
   280	
   281	        // Floyd-Warshall has O(n^3) complexity.
   282	        const double ops = static_cast<double>(numNodes) * numNodes * numNodes;
   283	        const double gflops = computationTime > 0.0
   284	                                  ? ops / computationTime / 1e9
   285	                                  : 0.0;
   286	        printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
   287	
   288	        if (printResults) {
   289	            print_results_int(globalDist, "DistanceMatrix");
   290	        }
   291	
   292	        if (validate) {
   293	            printf("Validating result...\n");
   294	            const bool valid = validateResult(globalDist, numNodes);
   295	
   296	            if (valid) {
   297	                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   298	            } else {
   299	                printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   300	            }
   301	            MPI_Finalize();
   302	            return valid ? 0 : 1;
   303	        }
   304	    }
   305	
   306	    MPI_Finalize();
   307	    return 0;

-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
-                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
-                   const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                   const size_t numNodes,
+                   const size_t firstColumn,
+                   const size_t numColumns,
+                   const std::vector<size_t>& columnStarts,
+                   const int rank) {
+    // The matrix is distributed in contiguous blocks of columns.  For each k,
+    // the owner broadcasts D[:,k], after which every rank updates its columns
+    // independently.  This is the column-major equivalent of the original
+    // i/j loop nest and requires only O(n) communication per iteration.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumn(numNodes);
+
     for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(
+            std::upper_bound(columnStarts.begin(), columnStarts.end(), k) -
+            columnStarts.begin() - 1);
+
+        if (rank == owner) {
+            const size_t localPivotColumn = k - firstColumn;
+            std::memcpy(pivotColumn.data(),
+                        dist.data() + localPivotColumn * numNodes,
+                        numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));
+        }
+
+        MPI_Bcast(pivotColumn.data(), static_cast<int>(numNodes), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                  owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+        for (size_t localColumn = 0; localColumn < numColumns; ++localColumn) {
+            unsigned int* distanceColumn = dist.data() + localColumn * numNodes;
+            unsigned int* pathColumn = path.data() + localColumn * numNodes;
+            const unsigned int distIK = distanceColumn[k];
+
             for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivotColumn[j];
+                if (newDist < distanceColumn[j]) {
+                    distanceColumn[j] = newDist;
+                    pathColumn[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
                 }
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
+size_t columnStart(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int worldSize) {

+    const size_t extraColumns = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);

+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extraColumns);

+    const size_t extraColumns = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    return baseColumns + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraColumns ? 1 : 0);

+        const size_t processOffset = columnStart(numNodes, process, worldSize) * numNodes;
+        if (processCount > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            processOffset > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            if (rank == 0) {
+                printf("The matrix distribution is too large for MPI counts\n");
+            }
+            MPI_Finalize();
             return 1;
         }
+        elementCounts[static_cast<size_t>(process)] = static_cast<int>(processCount);
+        elementDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(process)] = static_cast<int>(processOffset);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? initialDist.data() : nullptr,
+                 elementCounts.data(), elementDisplacements.data(), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                 dist.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    std::vector<unsigned int>().swap(initialDist);
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
+    }
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
+
+    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes, firstColumn, numColumns,
+                  columnStarts, rank);
+
+    const double elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double computationTime = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&elapsed, &computationTime, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    const bool needGlobalResult = printResults || validate;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> globalDist(
+        rank == 0 && needGlobalResult ? numNodes * numNodes : 0);
+    if (needGlobalResult) {
+        MPI_Gatherv(dist.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                    rank == 0 ? globalDist.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? elementCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? elementDisplacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const long long milliseconds = static_cast<long long>(computationTime * 1000.0);
+        printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);
+
+        // Floyd-Warshall has O(n^3) complexity.
+        const double ops = static_cast<double>(numNodes) * numNodes * numNodes;
+        const double gflops = computationTime > 0.0
+                                  ? ops / computationTime / 1e9
+                                  : 0.0;
+        printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results_int(globalDist, "DistanceMatrix");
+        }
+
+        if (validate) {
+            printf("Validating result...\n");
+            const bool valid = validateResult(globalDist, numNodes);
+
+            if (valid) {
+                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {
+                printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+            }
+            MPI_Finalize();
+            return valid ? 0 : 1;
+        }
     }
-    
+
+    MPI_Finalize();
     return 0;
 }

Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelization.

- Balanced column decomposition with per-iteration pivot broadcasts.
- Distributed distance/path matrices; root gathers only for `-r` or `-v`.
- MPI CMake integration; executable remains `floydwarshall`.
- Verified builds, validation, and identical hashes across 1–8 ranks.

+void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes,
+                          const size_t firstColumn, const size_t numColumns) {
+    // The original matrix is column-major and path[i,j] is initialized to j.
+    // Initialize only the columns owned by this rank.
+    for (size_t localColumn = 0; localColumn < numColumns; ++localColumn) {
+        const unsigned int column = static_cast<unsigned int>(firstColumn + localColumn);
+        unsigned int* pathColumn = path.data() + localColumn * numNodes;
+        std::fill_n(pathColumn, numNodes, column);
     }

+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                   const size_t numNodes,
+                   const size_t firstColumn,
+                   const size_t numColumns,
+                   const std::vector<size_t>& columnStarts,
+                   const int rank) {
+    // The matrix is distributed in contiguous blocks of columns.  For each k,
+    // the owner broadcasts D[:,k], after which every rank updates its columns
+    // independently.  This is the column-major equivalent of the original
+    // i/j loop nest and requires only O(n) communication per iteration.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumn(numNodes);
+
     for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {

-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(
+            std::upper_bound(columnStarts.begin(), columnStarts.end(), k) -
+            columnStarts.begin() - 1);
+
+        if (rank == owner) {
+            const size_t localPivotColumn = k - firstColumn;
+            std::memcpy(pivotColumn.data(),
+                        dist.data() + localPivotColumn * numNodes,
+                        numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));
+        }
+
+        MPI_Bcast(pivotColumn.data(), static_cast<int>(numNodes), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                  owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+        for (size_t localColumn = 0; localColumn < numColumns; ++localColumn) {
+            unsigned int* distanceColumn = dist.data() + localColumn * numNodes;
+            unsigned int* pathColumn = path.data() + localColumn * numNodes;

+                if (newDist < distanceColumn[j]) {
+                    distanceColumn[j] = newDist;
+                    pathColumn[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
                 }
             }
         }
     }

+size_t columnStart(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int worldSize) {
+    const size_t baseColumns = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t extraColumns = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    return static_cast<size_t>(rank) * baseColumns +
+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extraColumns);
+}
+
+size_t columnCount(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int worldSize) {
+    const size_t baseColumns = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t extraColumns = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    return baseColumns + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraColumns ? 1 : 0);
+}
